# Commands · M · part 5

1,510 entries, memlin-status to merge-and-create-branch. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **memlin-status**: Show Memlin status: call the `memlin_status` MCP tool (no arguments) and relay its `summary` field verbatim. It reports auth (access-token presence, expiry + refresh state), the bound account and project, MCP routing/host, last sync, and pending local changes — read from `~/.config/memlin/` by the local stdio MCP server. Prefer this tool over a terminal command. If the tool is unavailable, direct ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-status.md))
- **memlin-sync**: Pull latest team/project memory and skills from Memlin, then push any local changes. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-sync.md))
- **memlin-sync**: Run a full bidirectional Memlin sync for this project: execute `memlin sync` in the terminal. Report what was pulled and what was pushed. If conflicts surface (the server has a newer version of a document also edited locally), surface them — do not silently overwrite. ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-sync.md))
- **memlin-upload**: Save a screenshot, report, log, or other file to the existing Memlin Library for review. ([memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/memlin/commands/memlin-upload.md))
- **memlin-upload**: Save actual screenshots, reports, logs, and other generated files to the existing Memlin Library with `memlin upload &lt;path&gt; --role screenshot|report|log|output|reference --json`. Markdown with `--role report` becomes an editable versioned file. Files default to the linked project, or private without a project. The receipt identifies the exact resource, version, and verified hash. The command refus ([memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/memlin-ai/memlin-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memlin-upload.md))

- **memo-citations**: Deterministic citation-coverage critic for the memo skill. Scans the body markdown of the latest &lt;thread&gt;.{N}/ version dir for unhooked load-bearing claims (numeric, named-author, quantitative summary, date-pinned events) and broken \cite{} / [@] keys; writes a typed _review.json to the &lt;thread&gt;.{N}.citations/ sibling for the critics aggregator. Optional, non-blocking, idempotent. ([2AMLogic/anvil](https://github.com/2AMLogic/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memo/commands/memo-citations.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **memo-comprehension**: Cold-reader comprehension critic. Reads ONLY the memo body (blind — no BRIEF, no research, no rubric, no prior reviews), answers a fixed questionnaire in its own words, and diffs its answers against the intended message. Findings-only, non-gating. ([2AMLogic/anvil](https://github.com/2AMLogic/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memo/commands/memo-comprehension.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)

Idempotent re-run path for already-migrated threads (or after the operator edits BRIEF.md §Sources to add new entries). Wraps the seed_refs_from_brief helper that anvil:memo-migrate auto-invokes as step 13; provided as a standalone command for the cohort of threads migrated before step 13 landed and for post-edit re-seeding.
